Q1. A work is planned to finish in 12 days. After 5 days of work, find the remaining fraction.
A. 7/5
B. 5/12
C. 12/7
D. 7/12
Answer: 7/12
Difficulty: Easy
Family: tw_basic_residual_work
Explanation:
Set up the rate relation for this case.
\[
R=1-\frac{t}{T}
\]
Put the given values into that relation.
\[
R=1-\frac{5}{12}
\]
Solve for the asked value.
\[
R=\frac{7}{12}=7/12
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
R=\frac{12-5}{12}=7/12
\]
Answer: 7/12

---

Q2. 18 workers complete a job in 8 days by working 5 hours daily. For 6 hours daily and 12 days, find the required workers.
A. 18 workers
B. 13 workers
C. 10 workers
D. 27 workers
Answer: 10 workers
Difficulty: Medium
Family: tw_man_days_hours_basic
Explanation:
Set up the rate relation for this case.
\[
M_1D_1H_1=M_2D_2H_2
\]
Put the given values into that relation.
\[
18\times 8\times 5=M_2\times 12\times 6
\]
Solve for the asked value.
\[
M_2=10
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
M_2=\frac{18\times 8\times 5}{12\times 6}=10
\]
Answer: 10 workers

---

Q3. Printers A, B and C have hourly outputs 12, 15, 18 pages. Find the combined output in 6 hours.
A. 273 pages
B. 270 pages
C. 324 pages
D. 275 pages
Answer: 270 pages
Difficulty: Medium
Family: tw_printer_job_queue
Explanation:
Write output as rate multiplied by active time.
\[
O=\sum r_it_i
\]
Add the outputs of all active agents.
\[
O=12\times 6+15\times 6+18\times 6
\]
Simplify the total output.
\[
O=270
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
O=(r_1+r_2+r_3)t
\]
Answer: 270 pages

---

Q4. The full team takes 8 days. Removing known rates with individual times 20 days, find the remaining worker's time.
A. 8 1/3 days
B. 13 1/3 days
C. 14 1/3 days
D. 10 days
Answer: 13 1/3 days
Difficulty: Medium
Family: tw_pairwise_total_abc
Explanation:
Set up the rate relation for this case.
\[
r_X=r_{team}-\sum r_i
\]
Put the given values into that relation.
\[
r_X=\frac{1}{8}-\frac{1}{20}
\]
Solve for the asked value.
\[
T_X=13\frac{1}{3}
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T_X=1/r_X
\]
Answer: 13 1/3 days

---

Q6. A tank is filled for one hour at 12 units/hour and then drained for one hour at 5 units/hour repeatedly. Capacity is 60 units. Find completion time.
A. 16 1/3 hours
B. 15 1/3 hours
C. 11 1/3 hours
D. 20.42 hours
Answer: 16 1/3 hours
Difficulty: Hard
Family: pc_alternating_fill_empty
Explanation:
Write filling rates as positive and emptying rates as negative.
\[
W_{cycle}=12-5
\]
Find the effective tank rate.
\[
60\ units,\ cycle=7
\]
Divide the required tank work by that rate.
\[
T=16\frac{1}{3}
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T=C_f+t_f
\]
Answer: 16 1/3 hours

---

Q7. A completes \(\frac{1}{3}\) of a work in 9 days. Find the time needed for the whole work.
A. 3 days
B. 32 days
C. 27 days
D. 12 days
Answer: 27 days
Difficulty: Easy
Family: tw_work_fraction_days
Explanation:
Set up the rate relation for this case.
\[
T=d\times \frac{q}{p}
\]
Put the given values into that relation.
\[
T=9\times \frac{3}{1}
\]
Solve for the asked value.
\[
T=27
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T=9\div \frac{1}{3}=27
\]
Answer: 27 days

---

Q8. A starts a job alone and works for 4 days. A and B then finish it together. A alone takes 20 days and B alone takes 30 days. Find the total time.
A. 17 3/5 days
B. 12 3/5 days
C. 20 2/5 days
D. 13 3/5 days
Answer: 13 3/5 days
Difficulty: Medium
Family: tw_delayed_join
Explanation:
Split the work into timeline phases.
\[
W_1=r_At_1,\ T_2=\frac{W-W_1}{r_A+r_B}
\]
Subtract the work already completed.
\[
W_1=6\times 4,\quad R=120-24
\]
Use the active rate for the remaining phase.
\[
T=4+\frac{96}{6+4}=13.6
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T=4+\frac{120-6\times 4}{6+4}
\]
Answer: 13 3/5 days

---

Q9. A contract pays ₹6000 with a bonus of ₹250 per day for early completion. The work is completed 3 days early. Find the earning.
A. ₹8437.5
B. ₹6750
C. ₹6748
D. ₹6756
Answer: ₹6750
Difficulty: Medium
Family: tw_contract_penalty_bonus
Explanation:
Convert each person's work into contribution units.
\[
E=base+bonus
\]
Use contribution ratio for the share.
\[
E=6000+3\times 250
\]
Apply the wage or contract amount.
\[
E=6750
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
E=base+adjustment
\]
Answer: ₹6750

---

Q10. A phased job has 120 work units. Known phases complete 36 units. The last phase rate is 8 units per day. Find its duration.
A. 10 1/2 days
B. 7 1/2 days
C. 15 1/2 days
D. 7.88 days
Answer: 10 1/2 days
Difficulty: Hard
Family: tw_phase_state_reconstruction
Explanation:
Set up the rate relation for this case.
\[
x=\frac{W-W_1}{r}
\]
Put the given values into that relation.
\[
x=\frac{120-36}{8}
\]
Solve for the asked value.
\[
x=10.5
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
x=\frac{W_l}{r}
\]
Answer: 10 1/2 days

Q11. A pipe supplies 25 litres per minute. How much water is supplied in 20 minutes?
A. 500 litres
B. 750 litres
C. 499 litres
D. 504 litres
Answer: 500 litres
Difficulty: Easy
Family: pc_tank_capacity_from_rate
Explanation:
Write filling rates as positive and emptying rates as negative.
\[
C=rt
\]
Find the effective tank rate.
\[
C=25\times 20
\]
Divide the required tank work by that rate.
\[
C=500
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
C=r\times t
\]
Answer: 500 litres

Q12. In a phased job, the first phase lasts 5 days. Individual times of A, B and C are 24, 30 and 30 days. After that, B is replaced by C. Find total time.
A. 16 2/3 days
B. 19 1/3 days
C. 14 1/3 days
D. 13 1/3 days
Answer: 13 1/3 days
Difficulty: Medium
Family: tw_replacement_worker
Explanation:
Split the work into timeline phases.
\[
r_1t_1+r_2t_2=W
\]
Subtract the work already completed.
\[
W_1=9\times 5
\]
Use the active rate for the remaining phase.
\[
T=5+\frac{75}{9}=13\frac{1}{3}
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T=t_1+\frac{W-W_1}{r_2}
\]
Answer: 13 1/3 days

---

Q13. One team completes 18 units per day while another undoes 3 units per day. For 120 units, find the net completion time.

\[
T=\frac{W}{r_+-r_-}

Put the given values into that relation.
\[
T=\frac{120}{18-3}
\]
Solve for the asked value.
\[
T=8
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T=\frac{W}{r_n}
\]
Answer: 8 days

Q14. Pair times are AB=10 days, BC=12 days and CA=15 days. Find the time taken by A alone.
A. 28 4/5 days
B. 24 days
C. 29 days
D. 27 days
Answer: 24 days
Difficulty: Hard
Family: tw_pairwise_ab_bc_ac
Explanation:
Set up the rate relation for this case.
\[
2(A+B+C)=(A+B)+(B+C)+(C+A)
\]
Put the given values into that relation.
\[
AB=10,\ BC=12,\ CA=15
\]
Solve for the asked value.
\[
T_A=24
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
A=\frac{AB+AC-BC}{2}
\]
Answer: 24 days

---

Q16. A job needs 10 men for 15 days at 8 hours per day. Find the men required for 20 days at 6 hours per day.
A. 10 workers
B. 13.33 workers
C. 15 workers
D. 13 workers
Answer: 10 workers
Difficulty: Medium
Family: tw_farm_harvest_workers
Explanation:
Write output as rate multiplied by active time.
\[
M_1D_1H_1=M_2D_2H_2
\]
Add the outputs of all active agents.
\[
10\times 15\times 8=M_2\times 20\times 6
\]
Simplify the total output.
\[
M_2=10
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
M_2=\frac{10\times 15\times 8}{20\times 6}=10
\]
Answer: 10 workers

Q19. A contract wage of ₹9000 is divided in proportion to contributions 15:24. Find the required share.
A. ₹3467.54
B. ₹3463.54
C. ₹2769.23
D. ₹3461.54
Answer: ₹3461.54
Difficulty: Medium
Family: tw_wage_efficiency_ratio
Explanation:
Convert each person's work into contribution units.
\[
Share=\frac{c_i}{\sum c}\times W
\]
Use contribution ratio for the share.
\[
Share=\frac{15}{39}\times 9000
\]
Apply the wage or contract amount.
\[
Share=3461.54
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
Share=contribution\ ratio\times wage
\]
Answer: ₹3461.54

Q20. A job has 360 units. 8 workers finish 80 units, and 8 days are left. Find the extra workers required.
A. 27 workers
B. 30 workers
C. 31 workers
D. 21.6 workers
Answer: 27 workers
Difficulty: Hard
Family: tw_contract_deadline_extra_workers
Explanation:
Split the work into timeline phases.
\[
N_{extra}=\left\lceil\frac{R}{r\times t}\right\rceil-N_c
\]
Subtract the work already completed.
\[
N_{extra}=\left\lceil\frac{280}{1\times 8}\right\rceil-8
\]
Use the active rate for the remaining phase.
\[
N_{extra}=27
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
N_{extra}=N_r-N_c
\]
Answer: 27 workers

---

Q21. Pipes A and B fill a tank in 12 hours and 18 hours respectively. Both are opened together, but A is closed after 2 hours. Find the total time to fill the tank.
A. 12 hours
B. 19 hours
C. 15 hours
D. 16 hours
Answer: 15 hours
Difficulty: Medium
Family: pc_pipe_closed_before_completion
Explanation:
Split the work into timeline phases.
\[
W_1=(r_A+r_B)t_1,\quad T=t_1+\frac{W-W_1}{r_B}
\]
Subtract the work already completed.
\[
W_1=(3+2)\times 2
\]
Use the active rate for the remaining phase.
\[
T=2+\frac{26}{2}=15
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T=t_1+\frac{W-W_1}{r_B}
\]
Answer: 15 hours

---

\]

\[
R=360-10\times 6
\]
Use the active rate for the remaining phase.
\[
D=\frac{300}{8}-\frac{300}{10}
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
D=T_a-T_p
\]
Answer: 7 1/2 days

Q23. 600 person-days of food are available. 24 people consume it for 6 days, then the group becomes 30. Find how many more days the food will last.
A. 13 1/5 days
B. 11 2/5 days
C. 10 1/5 days
D. 15 1/5 days
Answer: 15 1/5 days
Difficulty: Medium
Family: tw_food_remaining_stock
Explanation:
Convert the stock into consumption units.
\[
D=\frac{S-consumed}{people}
\]
Subtract the stock already consumed.
\[
D=\frac{600-24\times 6}{30}
\]
Divide the remaining stock by the new daily use.
\[
D=15.2
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
D=\frac{S_l}{P_2}
\]
Answer: 15 1/5 days

Q24. Two workers have efficiencies 5:7. Their combined time is 8 days. Find A's individual time.
A. 19 1/5 days
B. 20 days
C. 11 1/5 days
D. 5.71 days
Answer: 19 1/5 days
Difficulty: Medium
Family: tw_efficiency_ratio_scaling
Explanation:
Set up the rate relation for this case.
\[
W=(a+b)T
\]
Put the given values into that relation.
\[
W=(5+7)\times 8
\]
Solve for the asked value.
\[
T_A=\frac{96}{5}=19.2
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T_A=\frac{(a+b)T}{a}=\frac{(5+7)8}{5}=19.2
\]
Answer: 19 1/5 days

Q25. A and B start a work together. A alone takes 8 days and B alone takes 12 days. A leaves after 4 days; B finishes the rest. Find the total time.
A. 8 days
B. 6 days
C. 11 days
D. 4 1/2 days
Answer: 6 days
Difficulty: Medium
Family: tw_forward_leave
Explanation:
Split the work into timeline phases.
\[
W=(r_A+r_B)t_1+r_Bt_2
\]
Subtract the work already completed.
\[
W_1=(9+6)\times 4
\]
Use the active rate for the remaining phase.
\[
T=4+\frac{12}{6}=6
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T=4+\frac{W-W_1}{B}
\]
Answer: 6 days

Q27. Pipe A fills a tank in 20 hours. Pipes A and B together fill it in 10 hours. Find the time taken by B alone.
A. 20 hours
B. 16 hours
C. 30 hours
D. 18 hours
Answer: 20 hours
Difficulty: Medium
Family: pc_find_pipe_rate_from_net_time
Explanation:
Write filling rates as positive and emptying rates as negative.
\[
\frac{1}{B}=\frac{1}{T}-\frac{1}{A}
\]
Find the effective tank rate.
\[
\frac{1}{B}=\frac{1}{10}-\frac{1}{20}
\]
Divide the required tank work by that rate.
\[
B=20
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
B=\frac{AT}{A-T}
\]
Answer: 20 hours

Q29. A pipe fills a tank in 12 hours. With a leak, it fills in 18 hours. Find the time in which the leak alone empties the full tank.
A. 42 hours
B. 36 hours
C. 33 hours
D. 28 4/5 hours
Answer: 36 hours
Difficulty: Medium
Family: pc_leak_hidden_rate
Explanation:
Write filling rates as positive and emptying rates as negative.
\[
\frac{1}{L}=\frac{1}{F}-\frac{1}{T}
\]
Find the effective tank rate.
\[
\frac{1}{L}=\frac{1}{12}-\frac{1}{18}
\]
Divide the required tank work by that rate.
\[
L=36
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
L=\frac{FT}{T-F}
\]
Answer: 36 hours

Q30. In the final cycle the work may finish before both turns are over. A and B produce 6 and 5 units per turn. Total work is 90 units.
A. 11 1/3 days
B. 16 1/3 days
C. 15 1/3 days
D. 20.42 days
Answer: 16 1/3 days
Difficulty: Hard
Family: tw_terminal_overshoot_cycle
Explanation:
Find the work completed in one full cycle.
\[
W_{cycle}=\sum r_i
\]
Count full cycles before the last turn.
\[
W_{cycle}=6+5
\]
Add only the needed part of the final turn.
\[
T=16\frac{1}{3}
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T=cycles+final\ turn
\]
Answer: 16 1/3 days

---

Q31. If 30 people can use a food stock for 36 days, find the days for 40 people.
A. 30 days
B. 33 days
C. 33 3/4 days
D. 27 days
Answer: 27 days
Difficulty: Easy
Family: tw_food_resource_basic
Explanation:
Convert the stock into consumption units.
\[
M_1D_1=M_2D_2
\]
Subtract the stock already consumed.
\[
30\times 36=40\times D_2
\]
Divide the remaining stock by the new daily use.
\[
D_2=27
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
D_2=\frac{30\times 36}{40}
\]
Answer: 27 days

Q38. Two workers take turns day by day. The first does 6 units and the second does 4 units in a day. How long will 60 units take?
A. 7 days
B. 11 days
C. 12 days
D. 15 days
Answer: 12 days
Difficulty: Hard
Family: tw_alternating_days_two_workers
Explanation:
Find the work completed in one full cycle.
\[
W_{cycle}=\sum r_i
\]
Count full cycles before the last turn.
\[
W_{cycle}=6+4
\]
Add only the needed part of the final turn.
\[
T=12
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T=cycles+final\ turn
\]
Answer: 12 days

Q42. A and B work on a job but leave before completion by 2 and 3 days respectively. Their alone times are 8 and 24 days. Find total time.

B. 14 1/4 days
C. 8 1/4 days
D. 6 3/5 days
Answer: 8 1/4 days
Difficulty: Hard
Family: tw_backward_leave
Explanation:

\[
r_A(T-a)+r_B(T-b)=W
\]
Put the given values into that relation.
\[
15(T-2)+5(T-3)=120
\]
Solve for the asked value.
\[
T=8.25
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T=\frac{W+A\times 2+B\times 3}{A+B}
\]
Answer: 8 1/4 days

Q43. A completes a job in 15 days, while B completes it in 30 days. Find the time required when both work together.

Family: tw_basic_combined_work

\[

\]

\]

\[
T=\frac{15\times 30}{15+30}=10
\]
Answer: 10 days

Q45. Pipes A and B fill a tank in 12 hours and 18 hours. A leak empties the full tank in 36 hours. Find the time to fill the tank when all are open.

B. 14 hours
C. 10 4/5 hours
D. 9 hours
Answer: 9 hours
Difficulty: Medium
Family: pc_two_fillers_one_empty
Explanation:

\[

\]

\[
r=1/12+1/18-1/36
\]
Divide the required tank work by that rate.
\[
T=9
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T=\frac{W_l}{r_n}
\]
Answer: 9 hours

Q51. After a delay, 100 of 300 work units are done by 10 workers. To meet a 8-day deadline, find extra workers.

Answer: 15 workers
Difficulty: Hard
Family: tw_worker_added_to_meet_deadline
Explanation:

\[

\]
Subtract the work already completed.
\[
N_{extra}=\left\lceil\frac{200}{1\times 8}\right\rceil-10
\]
Use the active rate for the remaining phase.
\[
N_{extra}=15
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
N_{extra}=N_r-N_c
\]
Answer: 15 workers

Q55. 18 men working 5 hours a day finish a work in 8 days. How many men are needed to finish it in 12 days working 6 hours a day?
A. 18 workers
B. 13 workers
C. 27 workers
D. 10 workers
Answer: 10 workers
Difficulty: Medium
Family: tw_road_construction_work
Explanation:
Write output as rate multiplied by active time.
\[
M_1D_1H_1=M_2D_2H_2
\]
Add the outputs of all active agents.
\[
18\times 8\times 5=M_2\times 12\times 6
\]
Simplify the total output.
\[
M_2=10
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
M_2=\frac{18\times 8\times 5}{12\times 6}=10
\]
Answer: 10 workers

C. 9 days
D. 18 days
Answer: 18 days
Difficulty: Medium
Family: tw_partial_completion_then_team
Explanation:
Set up the rate relation for this case.
\[
T=d\times \frac{q}{p}
\]
Put the given values into that relation.
\[
T=6\times \frac{3}{1}
\]
Solve for the asked value.
\[
T=18
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T=6\div \frac{1}{3}=18
\]
Answer: 18 days

Q79. A job has 300 units. 10 workers finish 80 units, and 12 days are left. Find the extra workers required.

C. 9 workers
D. 6.75 workers
Answer: 9 workers
Difficulty: Hard
Family: tw_worker_added_to_meet_deadline
Explanation:

\[
N_{extra}=\left\lceil\frac{R}{r\times t}\right\rceil-N_c
\]
Subtract the work already completed.
\[
N_{extra}=\left\lceil\frac{220}{1\times 12}\right\rceil-10
\]
Use the active rate for the remaining phase.
\[
N_{extra}=9
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
N_{extra}=N_r-N_c
\]
Answer: 9 workers

\]

D. 24 workers

\[

\]

\[

\]

\[
M_2=8
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
M_2=\frac{16\times 12\times 6}{18\times 8}=8
\]
Answer: 8 workers

\]

Explanation:
Write output as rate multiplied by active time.
\[
M_1D_1H_1=M_2D_2H_2
\]
Add the outputs of all active agents.
\[
15\times 16\times 6=M_2\times 12\times 8
\]
Simplify the total output.
\[
M_2=15
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
M_2=\frac{15\times 16\times 6}{12\times 8}=15
\]
Answer: 15 workers

T=cycles+final\ turn
\]
Answer: 7 1/3 days

Q137. Working alone, A takes 8 days and B takes 14 days for the same work. Find their combined time.
A. 11 days
B. 5.09 days
C. 22 days
D. 8 days
Answer: 5.09 days

Family: tw_basic_combined_work
Explanation:
Take the LCM as total work.
\[
W=LCM(8,14)=56
\]
Find each worker's one-period efficiency.
\[
A=56/8=7,\quad B=56/14=4
\]
Divide total work by the combined efficiency.
\[
T=\frac{56}{7+4}=5.09
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T=\frac{8\times 14}{8+14}=5.09
\]
Answer: 5.09 days

B. 3:5:6:3
C. 3:5:6
D. 6:5:3
Answer: 3:5:6
Difficulty: Medium
Family: tw_efficiency_chain
Explanation:
Set up the rate relation for this case.
\[
B_c
\]
Put the given values into that relation.
\[
LCM(5,5)=5
\]
Solve for the asked value.
\[
A:B:C=3:5:6
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
A:B:C=3:5:6
\]
Answer: 3:5:6

Q156. A worker does 15 units per day for 4 days, then slows to 12 units per day. Total work is 180 units. Find total time.
A. 11 days
B. 14 days
C. 17 1/2 days
D. 9 days
Answer: 14 days
Difficulty: Hard
Family: tw_productivity_decay
Explanation:
Set up the rate relation for this case.
\[
T=t_1+\frac{W-r_1t_1}{r_2}
\]
Put the given values into that relation.
\[
T=4+\frac{180-15\times 4}{12}
\]
Solve for the asked value.
\[
T=14
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T=t_1+t_2
\]
Answer: 14 days

Q157. Working alone, A takes 18 days and B takes 24 days for the same work. Find their combined time.
A. 18 days
B. 10.29 days
C. 42 days
D. 21 days
Answer: 10.29 days
Difficulty: Hard
Family: tw_lcm_hidden_total_work
Explanation:
Take the LCM as total work.
\[
W=LCM(18,24)=72
\]
Find each worker's one-period efficiency.
\[
A=72/18=4,\quad B=72/24=3
\]
Divide total work by the combined efficiency.
\[
T=\frac{72}{4+3}=10.29
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T=\frac{18\times 24}{18+24}=10.29
\]
Answer: 10.29 days

Q158. A and B together can finish a work in 16.36 days. Work is stopped for 3 days due to interruption. Find the calendar time.
A. 22.36 days
B. 24 1/5 days
C. 19.36 days
D. 25.36 days
Answer: 19.36 days
Difficulty: Medium
Family: tw_interrupted_work
Explanation:
Split the work into timeline phases.
\[
T_{calendar}=T_{active}+T_{idle}
\]
Subtract the work already completed.
\[
T_{active}=\frac{180}{6+5}
\]
Use the active rate for the remaining phase.
\[
T=T_{active}+3=19.36
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T=active+idle
\]
Answer: 19.36 days

Q159. Every cycle has three active turns with outputs 10, 6, 4 units. The job has 120 units. Find completion time.
A. 19 days
B. 18 days
C. 24 days
D. 22 1/2 days
Answer: 18 days
Difficulty: Hard
Family: tw_conditional_activation
Explanation:
Find the work completed in one full cycle.
\[
W_{cycle}=\sum r_i
\]
Count full cycles before the last turn.
\[
W_{cycle}=10+6+4
\]
Add only the needed part of the final turn.
\[
T=18
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T=cycles+final\ turn
\]
Answer: 18 days

\]

\]

\]

D. 8 days
Answer: 12 days
Difficulty: Easy
Family: tw_food_resource_basic
Explanation:
Convert the stock into consumption units.
\[
M_1D_1=M_2D_2
\]
Subtract the stock already consumed.
\[
20\times 24=40\times D_2
\]
Divide the remaining stock by the new daily use.
\[
D_2=12
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
D_2=\frac{20\times 24}{40}
\]
Answer: 12 days

Q191. A contract wage of ₹7200 is divided in proportion to contributions 24:36:40. Find the required share.
A. ₹1728
B. ₹1730
C. ₹1723
D. ₹2160
Answer: ₹1728
Difficulty: Medium
Family: tw_wage_distribution_efficiency
Explanation:
Convert each person's work into contribution units.
\[
Share=\frac{c_i}{\sum c}\times W
\]
Use contribution ratio for the share.
\[
Share=\frac{24}{100}\times 7200
\]
Apply the wage or contract amount.
\[
Share=1728
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
Share=contribution\ ratio\times wage
\]
Answer: ₹1728

Q192. One team completes 18 units per day while another undoes 3 units per day. For 120 units, find the net completion time.
A. 6 days
B. 8 days
C. 3 days
D. 11 days
Answer: 8 days
Difficulty: Medium
Family: tw_digging_filling_negative_work
Explanation:
Write output as rate multiplied by active time.
\[
T=\frac{W}{r_+-r_-}
\]
Add the outputs of all active agents.
\[
T=\frac{120}{18-3}
\]
Simplify the total output.
\[
T=8
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T=\frac{W}{r_n}
\]
Answer: 8 days

\]

\]
Answer: 8 workers

Q228. A job needs 15 men for 16 days at 6 hours per day. Find the men required for 12 days at 8 hours per day.
A. 18 workers
B. 15 workers
C. 11.25 workers
D. 20 workers
Answer: 15 workers
Difficulty: Medium
Family: tw_road_construction_work
Explanation:

\[
M_1D_1H_1=M_2D_2H_2
\]

\[
15\times 16\times 6=M_2\times 12\times 8
\]

\[
M_2=15
\]

\[
M_2=\frac{15\times 16\times 6}{12\times 8}=15
\]
Answer: 15 workers

Q234. Working alone, A takes 10 days and B takes 15 days for the same work. Find their combined time.
A. 6 days
B. 10 days
C. 12 1/2 days
D. 25 days
Answer: 6 days
Difficulty: Hard
Family: tw_lcm_hidden_total_work
Explanation:
Take the LCM as total work.
\[
W=LCM(10,15)=30
\]
Find each worker's one-period efficiency.
\[
A=30/10=3,\quad B=30/15=2
\]
Divide total work by the combined efficiency.
\[
T=\frac{30}{3+2}=6
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T=\frac{10\times 15}{10+15}=6
\]
Answer: 6 days

Q235. A job requires 8 working days by the team. Work remains paused for 3 days. Find total elapsed time.
A. 11 days
B. 7 days
C. 16 1/2 days
D. 12 days
Answer: 11 days
Difficulty: Medium
Family: tw_interrupted_work
Explanation:
Split the work into timeline phases.
\[
T_{calendar}=T_{active}+T_{idle}
\]
Subtract the work already completed.
\[
T_{active}=\frac{120}{10+5}
\]
Use the active rate for the remaining phase.
\[
T=T_{active}+3=11
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T=active+idle
\]
Answer: 11 days

Answer: 7 3/5 days

\[

\]
Answer: 7 3/5 days

C. 8:12
D. 12:20
Answer: 3:2
Difficulty: Easy
Family: tw_work_done_ratio_from_times
Explanation:
Set up the rate relation for this case.
\[
W_A:W_B=\frac{1}{8}:\frac{1}{12}
\]
Put the given values into that relation.
\[
W_A:W_B=12:8
\]
Solve for the asked value.
\[
W_A:W_B=3:2
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
E=3:2
\]
Answer: 3:2

---

Q267. After a delay, 120 of 300 work units are done by 12 workers. To meet a 8-day deadline, find extra workers.
A. 10 workers
B. 6 workers
C. 13.75 workers
D. 11 workers
Answer: 11 workers
Difficulty: Hard
Family: tw_contract_deadline_extra_workers
Explanation:
Split the work into timeline phases.
\[
N_{extra}=\left\lceil\frac{R}{r\times t}\right\rceil-N_c
\]
Subtract the work already completed.
\[
N_{extra}=\left\lceil\frac{180}{1\times 8}\right\rceil-12
\]
Use the active rate for the remaining phase.
\[
N_{extra}=11
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
N_{extra}=N_r-N_c
\]
Answer: 11 workers

Q268. A project has 360 work units. 12 workers work for 8 days, then 3 leave. Find the delay.

Q270. A and B begin a job together. After 2 days A leaves. If A and B alone take 18 and 10 days, find when the job ends.
A. 13 1/3 days
B. 8.89 days
C. 5.89 days
D. 12.89 days
Answer: 8.89 days
Difficulty: Medium
Family: tw_forward_leave
Explanation:
Split the work into timeline phases.
\[
W=(r_A+r_B)t_1+r_Bt_2
\]
Subtract the work already completed.
\[
W_1=(20+36)\times 2
\]
Use the active rate for the remaining phase.
\[
T=2+\frac{248}{36}=8.89
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T=2+\frac{W-W_1}{B}
\]
Answer: 8.89 days

Q271. Pipe A fills a tank in 20 hours. Pipes A and B together fill it in 6 hours. Find the time taken by B alone.
A. 6.43 hours
B. 3.57 hours
C. 11.57 hours
D. 8.57 hours
Answer: 8.57 hours
Difficulty: Medium
Family: pc_find_pipe_rate_from_net_time
Explanation:
Write filling rates as positive and emptying rates as negative.
\[
\frac{1}{B}=\frac{1}{T}-\frac{1}{A}
\]
Find the effective tank rate.
\[
\frac{1}{B}=\frac{1}{6}-\frac{1}{20}
\]
Divide the required tank work by that rate.
\[
B=8.57
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
B=\frac{AT}{A-T}
\]
Answer: 8.57 hours

---

Q272. A filling pipe takes 12 hours. Because of a leak, the tank fills in 17 hours. Find the leak time.
A. 51 hours
B. 40 4/5 hours
C. 41 4/5 hours
D. 46 4/5 hours
Answer: 40 4/5 hours
Difficulty: Medium
Family: pc_leak_hidden_rate
Explanation:
Write filling rates as positive and emptying rates as negative.
\[
\frac{1}{L}=\frac{1}{F}-\frac{1}{T}
\]
Find the effective tank rate.
\[
\frac{1}{L}=\frac{1}{12}-\frac{1}{17}
\]
Divide the required tank work by that rate.
\[
L=40.8
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
L=\frac{FT}{T-F}
\]
Answer: 40 4/5 hours

---

Q273. In the final cycle the work may finish before both turns are over. A and B produce 8 and 4 units per turn. Total work is 72 units.
A. 15 days
B. 13 days
C. 12 days
D. 18 days
Answer: 12 days
Difficulty: Hard
Family: tw_terminal_overshoot_cycle
Explanation:
Find the work completed in one full cycle.
\[
W_{cycle}=\sum r_i
\]
Count full cycles before the last turn.
\[
W_{cycle}=8+4
\]
Add only the needed part of the final turn.
\[
T=12
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T=cycles+final\ turn
\]
Answer: 12 days

Q274. A stock is enough for 30 people for 36 days. Find its duration for 30 people.
A. 39 days
B. 36 days
C. 28 4/5 days
D. 40 days
Answer: 36 days
Difficulty: Easy
Family: tw_food_resource_basic
Explanation:
Convert the stock into consumption units.
\[
M_1D_1=M_2D_2
\]
Subtract the stock already consumed.
\[
30\times 36=30\times D_2
\]
Divide the remaining stock by the new daily use.
\[
D_2=36
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
D_2=\frac{30\times 36}{30}
\]
Answer: 36 days

Q275. One team completes 18 units per day while another undoes 5 units per day. For 150 units, find the net completion time.
A. 9.54 days
B. 11.54 days
C. 17.54 days
D. 14.42 days
Answer: 11.54 days
Difficulty: Medium
Family: tw_digging_filling_negative_work
Explanation:
Write output as rate multiplied by active time.
\[
T=\frac{W}{r_+-r_-}
\]
Add the outputs of all active agents.
\[
T=\frac{150}{18-5}
\]
Simplify the total output.
\[
T=11.54
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T=\frac{W}{r_n}
\]
Answer: 11.54 days

Q280. A needs 15 days and B needs 30 days. In a joint job, A is absent for the last 2 days and B for the last 3 days. Find total duration.
A. 14 4/5 days
B. 12 1/3 days
C. 17 1/3 days
D. 10 1/3 days
Answer: 12 1/3 days
Difficulty: Hard
Family: tw_backward_leave
Explanation:
Set up the rate relation for this case.
\[
r_A(T-a)+r_B(T-b)=W
\]
Put the given values into that relation.
\[
8(T-2)+4(T-3)=120
\]
Solve for the asked value.
\[
T=12\frac{1}{3}
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T=\frac{W+A\times 2+B\times 3}{A+B}
\]
Answer: 12 1/3 days

Q281. Working alone, A takes 8 days and B takes 12 days for the same work. Find their combined time.
A. 20 days
B. 4 4/5 days
C. 10 days
D. 8 days
Answer: 4 4/5 days
Difficulty: Easy
Family: tw_basic_combined_work
Explanation:
Take the LCM as total work.
\[
W=LCM(8,12)=24
\]
Find each worker's one-period efficiency.
\[
A=24/8=3,\quad B=24/12=2
\]
Divide total work by the combined efficiency.
\[
T=\frac{24}{3+2}=4.8
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T=\frac{8\times 12}{8+12}=4.8
\]
Answer: 4 4/5 days

Split the work into timeline phases.
\[
N_{extra}=\left\lceil\frac{R}{r\times t}\right\rceil-N_c
\]
Subtract the work already completed.
\[
N_{extra}=\left\lceil\frac{240}{1\times 10}\right\rceil-12
\]
Use the active rate for the remaining phase.
\[
N_{extra}=12
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
N_{extra}=N_r-N_c
\]
Answer: 12 workers

---

\[

\]
Answer: 24 hours

Q341. Normally a tank fills in 15 hours, but with leakage it takes 21 hours. Find the leak's emptying time.
A. 48 1/2 hours
B. 78 3/4 hours
C. 52 1/2 hours
D. 53 1/2 hours
Answer: 52 1/2 hours
Difficulty: Medium
Family: pc_leak_hidden_rate
Explanation:
Write filling rates as positive and emptying rates as negative.
\[
\frac{1}{L}=\frac{1}{F}-\frac{1}{T}
\]
Find the effective tank rate.
\[
\frac{1}{L}=\frac{1}{15}-\frac{1}{21}
\]
Divide the required tank work by that rate.
\[
L=52.5
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
L=\frac{FT}{T-F}
\]
Answer: 52 1/2 hours

\]

Q343. A stock is enough for 20 people for 30 days. Find its duration for 25 people.

\]

Q349. A needs 24 days and B needs 30 days. In a joint job, A is absent for the last 2 days and B for the last 3 days. Find total duration.

B. 12.62 days
C. 19.78 days
D. 15.78 days
Answer: 15.78 days
Difficulty: Hard
Family: tw_backward_leave
Explanation:
Set up the rate relation for this case.
\[
r_A(T-a)+r_B(T-b)=W
\]
Put the given values into that relation.
\[
5(T-2)+4(T-3)=120
\]
Solve for the asked value.
\[
T=15.78
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T=\frac{W+A\times 2+B\times 3}{A+B}
\]
Answer: 15.78 days

Q350. Working alone, A takes 10 days and B takes 16 days for the same work. Find their combined time.
A. 13 days
B. 10 days
C. 26 days
D. 6.15 days
Answer: 6.15 days
Difficulty: Easy
Family: tw_basic_combined_work
Explanation:
Take the LCM as total work.
\[
W=LCM(10,16)=80
\]
Find each worker's one-period efficiency.
\[
A=80/10=8,\quad B=80/16=5
\]
Divide total work by the combined efficiency.
\[
T=\frac{80}{8+5}=6.15
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T=\frac{10\times 16}{10+16}=6.15
\]
Answer: 6.15 days

Put the given values into that relation.
\[
R=1-\frac{4}{30}
\]
Solve for the asked value.
\[
R=\frac{26}{30}=13/15
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
R=\frac{30-4}{30}=13/15
\]
Answer: 13/15

---

Q379. A team completes a work in 6 days. Known worker times are 12 days. Find the unknown worker's time.
A. 17 days
B. 12 days
C. 15 days
D. 14 2/5 days
Answer: 12 days
Difficulty: Medium
Family: tw_pairwise_total_abc
Explanation:
Set up the rate relation for this case.
\[
r_X=r_{team}-\sum r_i
\]
Put the given values into that relation.
\[
r_X=\frac{1}{6}-\frac{1}{12}
\]
Solve for the asked value.
\[
T_X=12
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T_X=1/r_X
\]
Answer: 12 days

Q381. A can finish a work in 8 days and B in 24 days. A works alone for 2 days, then B joins. Find the total time taken.
A. 12 1/2 days
B. 5 1/5 days
C. 6 1/2 days
D. 5 1/2 days
Answer: 6 1/2 days
Difficulty: Medium
Family: tw_delayed_join
Explanation:
Split the work into timeline phases.
\[
W_1=r_At_1,\ T_2=\frac{W-W_1}{r_A+r_B}
\]
Subtract the work already completed.
\[
W_1=15\times 2,\quad R=120-30
\]
Use the active rate for the remaining phase.
\[
T=2+\frac{90}{15+5}=6.5
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T=2+\frac{120-15\times 2}{15+5}
\]
Answer: 6 1/2 days

Q392. Pipe A fills a tank in 15 hours. Pipes A and B together fill it in 10 hours. Find the time taken by B alone.
A. 45 hours
B. 34 hours
C. 29 hours
D. 30 hours
Answer: 30 hours
Difficulty: Medium
Family: pc_find_pipe_rate_from_net_time
Explanation:
Write filling rates as positive and emptying rates as negative.
\[
\frac{1}{B}=\frac{1}{T}-\frac{1}{A}
\]
Find the effective tank rate.
\[
\frac{1}{B}=\frac{1}{10}-\frac{1}{15}
\]
Divide the required tank work by that rate.
\[
B=30
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
B=\frac{AT}{A-T}
\]
Answer: 30 hours

\[
W_1=(40+72)\times 2
\]
Use the active rate for the remaining phase.
\[
T=2+\frac{496}{72}=8.89
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T=2+\frac{W-W_1}{B}
\]
Answer: 8.89 days

Q454. Food for 20 people lasts 24 days. How many days will it last for 25 people?

\]
Answer: 19 1/5 days

Q455. One team completes 12 units per day while another undoes 4 units per day. For 120 units, find the net completion time.
A. 11 days
B. 16 days
C. 22 1/2 days
D. 15 days
Answer: 15 days
Difficulty: Medium
Family: tw_digging_filling_negative_work
Explanation:
Write output as rate multiplied by active time.
\[
T=\frac{W}{r_+-r_-}
\]
Add the outputs of all active agents.
\[
T=\frac{120}{12-4}
\]
Simplify the total output.
\[
T=15
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T=\frac{W}{r_n}
\]
Answer: 15 days

Q460. A and B work on a job but leave before completion by 2 and 3 days respectively. Their alone times are 15 and 30 days. Find total time.
A. 7 1/3 days
B. 12 1/3 days
C. 9 1/4 days
D. 13 1/3 days
Answer: 12 1/3 days
Difficulty: Hard
Family: tw_backward_leave
Explanation:
Set up the rate relation for this case.
\[
r_A(T-a)+r_B(T-b)=W
\]
Put the given values into that relation.
\[
12(T-2)+6(T-3)=180
\]
Solve for the asked value.
\[
T=12\frac{1}{3}
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T=\frac{W+A\times 2+B\times 3}{A+B}
\]
Answer: 12 1/3 days

Q461. A completes a job in 18 days, while B completes it in 24 days. Find the time required when both work together.
A. 10.29 days
B. 18 days
C. 42 days
D. 21 days
Answer: 10.29 days
Difficulty: Easy
Family: tw_basic_combined_work
Explanation:
Take the LCM as total work.
\[
W=LCM(18,24)=72
\]
Find each worker's one-period efficiency.
\[
A=72/18=4,\quad B=72/24=3
\]
Divide total work by the combined efficiency.
\[
T=\frac{72}{4+3}=10.29
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T=\frac{18\times 24}{18+24}=10.29
\]
Answer: 10.29 days

Q490. Food for 20 people lasts 36 days. How many days will it last for 25 people?
A. 27 4/5 days
B. 36 days
C. 28 4/5 days
D. 23 4/5 days
Answer: 28 4/5 days
Difficulty: Easy
Family: tw_food_resource_basic
Explanation:
Convert the stock into consumption units.
\[
M_1D_1=M_2D_2
\]
Subtract the stock already consumed.
\[
20\times 36=25\times D_2
\]
Divide the remaining stock by the new daily use.
\[
D_2=28.8
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
D_2=\frac{20\times 36}{25}
\]
Answer: 28 4/5 days

Q491. One team completes 15 units per day while another undoes 5 units per day. For 150 units, find the net completion time.
A. 15 days
B. 14 days
C. 20 days
D. 11 1/4 days
Answer: 15 days
Difficulty: Medium
Family: tw_digging_filling_negative_work
Explanation:
Write output as rate multiplied by active time.
\[
T=\frac{W}{r_+-r_-}
\]
Add the outputs of all active agents.
\[
T=\frac{150}{15-5}
\]
Simplify the total output.
\[
T=15
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T=\frac{W}{r_n}
\]
Answer: 15 days

Q492. A, B and C alone take 15, 30 and 36 days. For the first 3 days the initial phase runs, then B and C join. Find the total time.
A. 9.26 days
B. 7.41 days
C. 7.26 days
D. 13.26 days
Answer: 9.26 days
Difficulty: Hard
Family: tw_multi_phase_join_leave
Explanation:
Split the work into timeline phases.
\[
\sum r_it_i=W
\]
Subtract the work already completed.
\[
W_1=12\times 3
\]
Use the active rate for the remaining phase.
\[
T=3+\frac{144}{23}=9.26
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T=t_1+\frac{W-W_1}{r_2}
\]
Answer: 9.26 days

Q493. A works on the first day, B on the second day, and this pattern repeats. A and B do 6 and 4 units per day. Find the time for 120 units.
A. 28 days
B. 24 days
C. 19 1/5 days
D. 27 days
Answer: 24 days
Difficulty: Hard
Family: tw_alternating_days_two_workers
Explanation:
Find the work completed in one full cycle.
\[
W_{cycle}=\sum r_i
\]
Count full cycles before the last turn.
\[
W_{cycle}=6+4
\]
Add only the needed part of the final turn.
\[
T=24
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T=cycles+final\ turn
\]
Answer: 24 days

Q494. A team can complete a work in 20 days. It works for 5 days. What fraction of work is left?
A. 4/3
B. 3/4
C. 3/1
D. 1/4
Answer: 3/4
Difficulty: Easy
Family: tw_remaining_work_fraction
Explanation:
Set up the rate relation for this case.
\[
R=1-\frac{t}{T}
\]
Put the given values into that relation.
\[
R=1-\frac{5}{20}
\]
Solve for the asked value.
\[
R=\frac{15}{20}=3/4
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
R=\frac{20-5}{20}=3/4
\]
Answer: 3/4

Q495. After a delay, 60 of 360 work units are done by 10 workers. To meet a 10-day deadline, find extra workers.
A. 18 workers
B. 10 workers
C. 20 workers
D. 15 workers
Answer: 20 workers
Difficulty: Hard
Family: tw_worker_added_to_meet_deadline
Explanation:
Split the work into timeline phases.
\[
N_{extra}=\left\lceil\frac{R}{r\times t}\right\rceil-N_c
\]
Subtract the work already completed.
\[
N_{extra}=\left\lceil\frac{300}{1\times 10}\right\rceil-10
\]
Use the active rate for the remaining phase.
\[
N_{extra}=20
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
N_{extra}=N_r-N_c
\]
Answer: 20 workers

Q497. The combined time of A and B is 8 days. If A can finish alone in 20 days, find B's time.
A. 12 days
B. 13 1/3 days
C. 14 days
D. 28 days
Answer: 13 1/3 days
Difficulty: Medium
Family: tw_individual_from_combined
Explanation:
Set up the rate relation for this case.
\[
\frac{1}{B}=\frac{1}{T}-\frac{1}{A}
\]
Put the given values into that relation.
\[
\frac{1}{B}=\frac{1}{8}-\frac{1}{20}
\]
Solve for the asked value.
\[
B=13\frac{1}{3}
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
B=\frac{8\times 20}{20-8}=13\frac{1}{3}
\]
Answer: 13 1/3 days

Q498. A completes a job in 24 days, while B completes it in 10 days. Find the time required when both work together.
A. 17 days
B. 34 days
C. 10 days
D. 7.06 days
Answer: 7.06 days
Difficulty: Hard
Family: tw_lcm_hidden_total_work
Explanation:
Take the LCM as total work.
\[
W=LCM(24,10)=120
\]
Find each worker's one-period efficiency.
\[
A=120/24=5,\quad B=120/10=12
\]
Divide total work by the combined efficiency.
\[
T=\frac{120}{5+12}=7.06
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T=\frac{24\times 10}{24+10}=7.06
\]
Answer: 7.06 days

---

Q499. A job requires 8.18 working days by the team. Work remains paused for 2 days. Find total elapsed time.
A. 6.18 days
B. 15.27 days
C. 10.18 days
D. 11.18 days
Answer: 10.18 days
Difficulty: Medium
Family: tw_interrupted_work
Explanation:
Split the work into timeline phases.
\[
T_{calendar}=T_{active}+T_{idle}
\]
Subtract the work already completed.
\[
T_{active}=\frac{630}{35+42}
\]
Use the active rate for the remaining phase.
\[
T=T_{active}+2=10.18
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T=active+idle
\]
Answer: 10.18 days
